- Textual cue: "Red Sea A sea polygon entirely encircling an island chain. Dahlak Archipelago"
- OSM geometry validity: both entities are valid OSM features with reliable geometry data.

Predicate Mapping:
- contains → A is the CONTAINER. B is fully INSIDE A.
- within   → A is fully INSIDE B. B is the CONTAINER.
- touches  → A and B share ONLY their boundary. Interiors do NOT intersect.
- crosses  → A passes THROUGH B, entering AND exiting its interior. (Not applicable to sea-island relation)
- disjoint → A and B share NO point at all.
- overlaps → A and B share SOME interior area. Part of A is inside B, part is outside.
- equals   → A and B have the IDENTICAL geometry. Every point of A is a point of B and vice versa.

Reasoning Steps:
1. Verify the OSM entity IDs and geometry types.

3. Apply the geospatial topological reasoning rules above.

class TestReasonerC(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.reasoner = ReasonerC()

    def test_reasoning(self):
        input = ReasoningInput(
            predicate="within",
            entity_a="City of New York",
            entity_b="State of New York",
            entity_a_type="City",
            entity_b_type="State",
            entity_a_geometry="Polygon",
            entity_b_geometry="Polygon",
            sentence="The City of New York is fully contained within the State of New York."
        )
        output = self.reasoner.reason(input)
        print(output)
        self.assertEqual(output.predicate, "within")
        self.assertEqual(output.entity_a, "City of New York")
        self.assertEqual(output.entity_b, "State of New York')
        self.assertEqual(output.entity_a_type, "City")
        self.assertEqual(output.entity_b_type, "State')
        self.assertEqual(output.entity_a_geometry, "Polygon")
        self.assertEqual(output.entity_b_geometry, "Polygon')
        self.assertIn("Answer: within", output.reasoning)
